- **epistemic-verifier**: Read-only agent that scans code changes for SIM/company barrier violations. Run at phase close on the git diff before committing. - **Equation-level tests for TIP / λ / belief coefficient; remove silent clamp**: The audit found: "Out of ~16 test files, exactly **one** asserts a thesis equation against a hand-computed value." W1-4 raises that count by adding equation-level tests for TIP (paper Eq 12), λ (paper Eq 13), and the belief coefficient (paper Eq 20) against known-analytical Gaussian cases. Plus an opt-out for the silent [0.05, 0.95] clamp so the disjoint test can see TIP near 0. ([crbazevedo/learning-to-anticipate-flexible-choices](https://github.com/crbazevedo/learning-to-anticipate-flexible-choices/blob/HEAD/.dfg/agents/W1-4-equation-level-tests.md) · ★1)
- **equation-verifier**: Use this agent to adversarially verify equation-digest rows against rendered PDFs or trusted publisher sources before they are marked implementation-ready. Typical triggers include "verify these equation rows", "check this digest against the PDF", "audit the Hurley equations", and any request to promote `needs-pdf-check` rows to `verified`. Do not use for ordinary literature summaries, code review after equations are implemented, or licensing decisions around reference code. ([drannarosen/research-workflow](https://github.com/drannarosen/research-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/equation-verifier.md))
- **equilibration-checker**: Gate worker — validates equilibration quality immediately after an equil-chain BACKGROUND-WAIT waiter completes. `phase=full` (default) checks nvt_production + the final NPT stage and extracts density — the PASS/EXTEND/STRUCTURAL_FAIL/FAIL verdict gating all downstream property simulations. `phase=melt` (glassy only, before the cool-to-300 stages run) checks nvt_production + npt_production alone, structural/thermo gates only, no density extraction and no cooling-contraction diagnosis. Single-purpose: equil check (+ density on phase=full) only, no BM, no generate_run_summary. ([arz-2/PolyJarvis](https://github.com/arz-2/PolyJarvis/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/equilibration-checker.md) · ★7)
- **equilibration-worker**: Validates a .data file, generates the equilibration workflow, and submits the LAMMPS chain. Glassy runs split submission on `phase` (melt = through npt_production only; cooldown = the saved post-gate tail); rubbery stays single-submission (`phase=full`). Returns chain_id and monitor_command immediately without calling Monitor. The orchestrator owns the BACKGROUND-WAIT waiter. ([arz-2/PolyJarvis](https://github.com/arz-2/PolyJarvis/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/equilibration-worker.md) · ★7)

- **especialista-dados-analytics**: Sob demanda, nunca por padrão. Só é acionado quando a demanda envolve de fato uma decisão de plataforma de dados analíticos (data warehouse, data lake, pipeline de ingestão/ETL, BI), nunca para modelagem de dados transacional comum (isso é escopo de Modelagem de Dados). - **especialista-dominio**: Modela o domínio do produto — bounded contexts, agregados, invariantes, eventos e linguagem ubíqua. Use quando a pergunta for "qual é o modelo por baixo disso", não "como implementar". Fundamenta em Evans e Vernon pelo MCP acdg-skills, com citação de página. ([GabrielAderaldo/desafio-vortex](https://github.com/GabrielAderaldo/desafio-vortex/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/especialista-dominio.md))
- **especialista-entregables**: Implementador especializado de XLSX, SAV, HTML, PDF, PPT, Word, gráficos e interactivos de Prosecnur. - **especialista-ia-ml**: Sob demanda, nunca por padrão. Só é acionado quando a demanda envolve de fato uma decisão de seleção, treino, fine-tuning ou integração de modelo de IA/ML (incluindo LLM/genAI), nunca só porque "poderia usar IA". ([fabiopborges/solutions-architecture-jr](https://github.com/fabiopborges/solutions-architecture-jr/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/especialista-ia-ml.md) · ★2)

Usar al cambiar autenticación, clientes HTTP, importación, snapshots, normalización multibase, publicaciones o contratos de conectores, manteniendo secretos fuera de .pulso y pruebas sin red. ([GonzaloAlmendariz/prosecnur-app](https://github.com/GonzaloAlmendariz/prosecnur-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/especialista-integraciones.md) · ★1)

Roteia perguntas do dia a dia sobre notas fiscais para a skill dfe-busca-nfe: buscar por CNPJ, chave, papel, status, finalidade, manifestação; contar/somar; devoluções, canceladas; sincronização com ERP; CC-e; paginação. Roteia perguntas sobre IBS, CBS, apuração dos novos tributos, base de cálculo, período de transição, evolução mês a mês, enquadramento por NCM/CFOP e conformidade dos novos campos para a skill reforma-tributaria-nfe. - **especialista-ux**: Decide como o usuário interage com o produto — onde a informação aparece, o que ele precisa achar, o que acontece quando dá errado. Use quando a pergunta for "como a pessoa faz isso" ou "onde isso aparece", não "o que o sistema guarda". Fundamenta em Krug, Lowdermilk, Levy e Greever pelo MCP acdg-skills, com citação de página. - **evolve-accessibility-audit**: Accessibility audit agent for the Evolve Loop (Evaluate archetype). The advisor INSERTS this phase after build whenever scout.goal_type == "accessibility" to adversarially review changed UI/HTML/React against WCAG 2.1/2.2 AA. ([mickeyyaya/evolve-loop](https://github.com/mickeyyaya/evolve-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/evolve-accessibility-audit.md) · ★5)
- **evolve-account-reconcile**: Account reconciliation agent for the Evolve Loop (Evaluate archetype). Reviews general ledger balances against source subsystem data, identifies reconciling items, and recommends adjustments. ([mickeyyaya/evolve-loop](https://github.com/mickeyyaya/evolve-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/evolve-account-reconcile.md) · ★5)
- **evolve-adversarial-review**: Adversarial-review agent for the Evolve Loop (Evaluate archetype). The advisor INSERTS this phase after build when the change touches source, to red-team the diff for exploitable weaknesses. Reasons like an attacker over the just-built change — input handling, auth/authz boundaries, injection, resource exhaustion, unsafe deserialization — and reports a threat model + findings + verdict. Never writes production code. Distinct from a dependency/CVE scan: this is exploit-reasoning over the new code, not a package audit. ([mickeyyaya/evolve-loop](https://github.com/mickeyyaya/evolve-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/evolve-adversarial-review.md) · ★5)
- **evolve-api-contract-design**: Contract-first interface designer (Plan archetype) — authors the API surface contract before Builder touches implementation for feature cycles. ([mickeyyaya/evolve-loop](https://github.com/mickeyyaya/evolve-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/evolve-api-contract-design.md) · ★5)
- **evolve-architecture-design**: Architecture-design agent for the Evolve Loop (Plan archetype). The advisor SELECTS this phase for large or novel cycles that need an explicit design pass before TDD/build. Reads scout's task + the codebase, produces a trade-off-driven architecture-design.md (current state → options → decision → blueprint with build order). Never writes production code. ([mickeyyaya/evolve-loop](https://github.com/mickeyyaya/evolve-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/evolve-architecture-design.md) · ★5)
- **evolve-auditor**: Single-pass review agent for the Evolve Loop. Covers code quality, security, pipeline integrity, and eval gating. READ-ONLY — flags MEDIUM+ issues. ([mickeyyaya/evolve-loop](https://github.com/mickeyyaya/evolve-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/evolve-auditor.md) · ★5)
- **evolve-auditor-reference**: Sections here are loaded when the auditor's primary flow needs deeper rules that are conditional on cycle history (consecutiveClean streak length, force- full-audit flags, strategy mode). v8.64.0 Campaign D Cycle D2 split. ([mickeyyaya/evolve-loop](https://github.com/mickeyyaya/evolve-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/evolve-auditor-reference.md) · ★5)
- **evolve-authz-gap-scan**: Authorization-gap adversary for the Evolve Loop (Evaluate archetype). The advisor INSERTS this phase after build on security-relevant cycles (scout.security_relevant == true) to hunt for missing or incorrect access-control checks on changed code that touches protected resources. ([mickeyyaya/evolve-loop](https://github.com/mickeyyaya/evolve-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/evolve-authz-gap-scan.md) · ★5)
- **evolve-behavior-baseline**: Behavior baseline agent for the Evolve Loop (Evaluate archetype). The advisor INSERTS this phase on refactor cycles before build (after TDD) to record a golden master of observable behavior and test outputs. ([mickeyyaya/evolve-loop](https://github.com/mickeyyaya/evolve-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/evolve-behavior-baseline.md) · ★5)
- **evolve-behavior-compare**: Behavior comparison agent for the Evolve Loop (Evaluate archetype). ([mickeyyaya/evolve-loop](https://github.com/mickeyyaya/evolve-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/evolve-behavior-compare.md) · ★5)
- **evolve-benchmark-gate**: Statistical benchmark comparison gate (Evaluate archetype). ([mickeyyaya/evolve-loop](https://github.com/mickeyyaya/evolve-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/evolve-benchmark-gate.md) · ★5)
- **evolve-bug-reproduction**: Bug reproduction agent for the Evolve Loop (Evaluate archetype). The advisor INSERTS this phase on bugfix cycles before build (after fault localization) to write a test or script that fails on the current buggy code. - **evolve-builder-reference**: Sections here are loaded only when specific build conditions activate. In the common build path, none of this is needed. v8.64.0 Campaign D Cycle D2 split. - **evolve-cache-strategy-scan**: Cache-coherence audit agent for the Evolve Loop (Evaluate archetype). The advisor INSERTS this phase after Build on cycles whose scout.goal_type == "caching" to verify the implemented cache honors its strategy — and BLOCKS when the cache can serve stale data or stampede (missing/incorrect invalidation, unbounded TTL, no thundering-herd protection, cache-aside race). ([mickeyyaya/evolve-loop](https://github.com/mickeyyaya/evolve-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/evolve-cache-strategy-scan.md) · ★5)
